Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on electronics structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 306. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 983 with relations in degree 2.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 306 + O(1/n^983). Summing over all modes and applying the electronics convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
